This is the ColorWay Sports MLB daily uniform tracker: what every team actually wore, every game, every day, logged the morning after. Not just the City Connects and throwbacks, the whole wardrobe, home whites and road grays included, because you can't spot the patterns without the full record. Every matchup also gets a ColorWay grade for how the two uniforms looked sharing a field.

Wondering what jersey your team is wearing tonight? Our [MLB uniform schedule guide](/stories/mlb-uniform-schedule-2026) and the per-team schedule pages linked at the bottom of this tracker map what all 30 teams plan to wear and when, so you can call tonight's uniform before first pitch. This page is the receipts: what they actually put on.

## Saturday, July 11

A big 16-game Saturday, headlined by the Brewers and Pirates finally playing Friday's rained-out game as part of a doubleheader in Pittsburgh. Saturday brought the color the midweek slates lacked: City Connects for the Giants and Blue Jays, powder blue for the Brewers and Royals, the Cardinals' cream, the Astros' orange, and a stack of alternates. Slate average: 7.0 out of 10.

<div style="margin: 2em 0 0.6em; padding: 0.7em 1.1em; background: linear-gradient(135deg, #004687 0%, #1c1c1c 100%); border-radius: 12px 12px 0 0;">

  <span style="display: block; font-size: 1.15em; font-weight: 900; color: #ffffff; margin-top: 3px;">Royals Powder Blue</span>
</div>

Our pick from Saturday's slate: Kansas City's powder blue, one of the cleanest looks in the game, worn in Baltimore. Each day we spotlight one team's look straight from their own feed, so you see the uniform on the field and not just on the rack.

## Friday, July 10

Fourteen games, with Brewers at Pirates rained out and rescheduled as a Saturday doubleheader, a washout that also claimed the Pirates' Friday-home black City Connect. The rest of the league more than covered for it: eight City Connects hit the field in one night after two straight nights of zero, exactly the weekend pattern this tracker was built to prove. Only two home teams stayed in white, seven road teams stayed in gray, and 19 of the 28 uniforms on the field were alternates or City Connects. Slate average: 7.5 out of 10, the best-dressed night of the log so far.

## Thursday, July 9

The full 13-game slate is in the books. A mostly classic Thursday: a dozen home teams in white or cream, six road teams in the standard grays, and eight clubs reaching for an alternate. Almost all the color came from the road, where seven visiting teams broke out an alternate, from the Brewers' powder blue and Mariners' navy to the Diamondbacks' black and the Cubs' and Royals' royal blues, while Cincinnati supplied the night's only home alternate in its red. No City Connects. Slate average: 6.9 out of 10.

## The Pattern Engine: Running Uniform Counts

This is where the tracker earns its keep over time. Every look gets counted, and as the days stack up this section becomes the evidence file behind every "what jersey are they wearing today" question: how often each team wears each uniform, and which days of the week each alternate actually shows up. Four days in, here is the alternate board.

Two exceptions prove the rule. The Pirates' Friday-home black City Connect missed only because the game itself was rained out, and the Nationals were the lone club to skip their slot by choice, wearing the navy alternate instead of the District Blueprint. Friday is officially City Connect night in 2026, and the log now has the receipts.

## Frequently Asked Questions

**What is the MLB daily uniform tracker?**

It is a running log of what every MLB team actually wore in every game, updated each morning after the previous night's slate. Every jersey and uniform gets recorded, from home whites and road grays to alternates, City Connects, and throwbacks, and every matchup gets a ColorWay grade for how the two looks paired on the field.

**What jersey is my team wearing tonight?**

Open your team's page from the uniform schedule grid above. It maps every fixed jersey day the club runs, like Friday City Connects, Sunday creams, or powder blue day games, so you can call tonight's look before first pitch. Then check back here tomorrow morning, because this tracker logs the uniform every MLB team actually wore, every single day.

**How do I find out what jersey the Dodgers or Yankees are wearing tonight?**

The [Dodgers uniform schedule](/stories/dodgers-uniform-schedule-2026) and [Yankees uniform schedule](/stories/yankees-uniform-schedule-2026) pages predict tonight's jersey from each team's day-of-week rotation, and the daily log on this page records what they actually wore last night, from home whites to City Connects. The same goes for all 30 clubs through the team pages above, so the question has an answer both before and after every game.

**How do you know what uniform each team wore?**

We check every game the morning after using game photos and broadcast footage from the night before, then log both teams' uniforms. When something is ambiguous, like a City Connect versus a similar regular alternate, we verify against multiple dated game photos before recording it.

**How often do MLB teams wear their City Connect uniforms?**

Most City Connect uniforms follow a fixed weekly slot. The Pirates wear their all-black 2026 City Connect for Friday home games, the Reds wear their pinstriped City Connect 2.0 on Saturdays, and most other clubs use similar weekend anchors. The running counts on this page will show the real frequency as the season plays out.

**Do MLB teams always wear white at home and gray on the road?**

No, and that is exactly why this tracker exists. White at home and gray on the road is the default, but on the Friday, July 10 slate alone, 19 of the 28 uniforms on the field were alternates or City Connects. Some clubs, like the Phillies, follow a strict published rotation, while others swap in alternates whenever the mood or the starting pitcher calls for it.

**When do MLB teams wear their alternate jerseys?**

It varies by team. Some alternates have fixed days, like Friday reds or Sunday creams, while others are manager's choice or a starting pitcher's preference. That day-of-week data is exactly what this tracker is built to surface, and the pattern section above fills in with evidence as the days accumulate.

## The Bottom Line on the MLB Daily Uniform Tracker

Baseball puts on more uniforms than any other sport, and nobody logs what actually hits the field each night. That is the gap this page fills: every team, every game, every day, graded and counted, with the day-of-week patterns emerging as the record grows. Bookmark it, check it with your morning coffee, and when you want to know what your team wears next, the [full uniform schedule guide](/stories/mlb-uniform-schedule-2026) has the forward-looking answer.
